MC	<p>4c26_2d32</p> <p><b>Purpose:</b> Practice calculating Z-scores in Google Sheets using the population mean (&mu;, mu = 120) and population standard deviation (&sigma;, sigma = 15).</p><p>Follow these steps:</p><ol><li>Open the Google Sheet Template: <a href='https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1y-fVDSLoRPGuCABAD5gy-Ai4qzSFqqhnayOI4kMpPkQ/edit' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>Google Sheet Template</a></li><li>File &rarr; Make a copy.</li><li>Paste the table below into the sheet (two columns: Name and BP).</li><li>Use &mu; = 120 and &sigma; = 15 to compute Z for each BP.</li><li>Formula hint: <span style='font-family: monospace;'>(B2 - &mu;)/&sigma;</span>. Here <b>B2</b> means the first BP value in column B. After typing the formula in the first row, place your mouse on the small square in the bottom-right corner of the cell (the fill handle) and drag it down. The spreadsheet will automatically change B2 to B3 for the next row, then B4, and so on, so each patient's BP gets its own Z-score.</li><li><i>Pro Tip:</i> Advanced users can highlight the formula cell and the rows below it, then press &#8984;D (Mac) or Ctrl+D (Windows) to autofill the formula quickly.</li></ol><br/><table border='1' style='border-collapse:collapse; font-family: monospace; background-color:#eee;'><tr><th>Patient Name</th><th>Systolic BP (mmHg)</th></tr><tr><td>Adela</td><td>159</td></tr><tr><td>Bhargavi</td><td>100</td></tr><tr><td>Chanel</td><td>129</td></tr><tr><td>Daniella</td><td>125</td></tr><tr><td>Matthew</td><td>78</td></tr><tr><td>Nivedha</td><td>117</td></tr><tr><td>Sunana</td><td>139</td></tr><tr><td>Vishwa</td><td>114</td></tr></table><br/><p><b>Question:</b> Which named patient has a Z-score between -3 and -2 standard deviations?</p>	Adela with a BP of 159	Incorrect	Bhargavi with a BP of 100	Incorrect	Daniella with a BP of 125	Incorrect	Matthew with a BP of 78	Correct	Sunana with a BP of 139	Incorrect

The spreadsheet will automatically change B2 to B3 for the next row, then B4, and so on, so each patient's BP gets its own Z-score.</li><li><i>Pro Tip:</i> Advanced users can highlight the formula cell and the rows below it, then press &#8984;D (Mac) or Ctrl+D (Windows) to autofill the formula quickly.</li></ol><br/><table border='1' style='border-collapse:collapse; font-family: monospace; background-color:#eee;'><tr><th>Patient Name</th><th>Systolic BP (mmHg)</th></tr><tr><td>Abbie</td><td>128</td></tr><tr><td>Braulio</td><td>78</td></tr><tr><td>Deanna</td><td>118</td></tr><tr><td>Lindita</td><td>122</td></tr><tr><td>Mary</td><td>140</td></tr><tr><td>Nathi</td><td>159</td></tr><tr><td>Shalini</td><td>101</td></tr><tr><td>Yomara</td><td>114</td></tr></table><br/><p><b>Question:</b> Which named patient has a Z-score between +2 and +3 standard deviations?</p>	Abbie with a BP of 128	Incorrect	Braulio with a BP of 78	Incorrect	Lindita with a BP of 122	Incorrect	Mary with a BP of 140	Incorrect	Nathi with a BP of 159	Correct

The spreadsheet will automatically change B2 to B3 for the next row, then B4, and so on, so each patient's BP gets its own Z-score.</li><li><i>Pro Tip:</i> Advanced users can highlight the formula cell and the rows below it, then press &#8984;D (Mac) or Ctrl+D (Windows) to autofill the formula quickly.</li></ol><br/><table border='1' style='border-collapse:collapse; font-family: monospace; background-color:#eee;'><tr><th>Patient Name</th><th>Systolic BP (mmHg)</th></tr><tr><td>Alejandra</td><td>123</td></tr><tr><td>Cody</td><td>113</td></tr><tr><td>Joss</td><td>79</td></tr><tr><td>Rutvi</td><td>100</td></tr><tr><td>Sabrina</td><td>117</td></tr><tr><td>Tatiana</td><td>127</td></tr><tr><td>Vibhuti</td><td>140</td></tr><tr><td>Yomara</td><td>161</td></tr></table><br/><p><b>Question:</b> Which named patient has a Z-score between -2 and -1 standard deviations?</p>	Cody with a BP of 113	Incorrect	Joss with a BP of 79	Incorrect	Rutvi with a BP of 100	Correct	Sabrina with a BP of 117	Incorrect	Yomara with a BP of 161	Incorrect

The spreadsheet will automatically change B2 to B3 for the next row, then B4, and so on, so each patient's BP gets its own Z-score.</li><li><i>Pro Tip:</i> Advanced users can highlight the formula cell and the rows below it, then press &#8984;D (Mac) or Ctrl+D (Windows) to autofill the formula quickly.</li></ol><br/><table border='1' style='border-collapse:collapse; font-family: monospace; background-color:#eee;'><tr><th>Patient Name</th><th>Systolic BP (mmHg)</th></tr><tr><td>Alyaa</td><td>126</td></tr><tr><td>Dima</td><td>119</td></tr><tr><td>Foramben</td><td>139</td></tr><tr><td>Isaac</td><td>156</td></tr><tr><td>Jeanette</td><td>118</td></tr><tr><td>Kaushal</td><td>126</td></tr><tr><td>Mit</td><td>100</td></tr><tr><td>Suchi</td><td>87</td></tr></table><br/><p><b>Question:</b> Which named patient has a Z-score between -3 and -2 standard deviations?</p>	Alyaa with a BP of 126	Incorrect	Foramben with a BP of 139	Incorrect	Isaac with a BP of 156	Incorrect	Mit with a BP of 100	Incorrect	Suchi with a BP of 87	Correct

The spreadsheet will automatically change B2 to B3 for the next row, then B4, and so on, so each patient's BP gets its own Z-score.</li><li><i>Pro Tip:</i> Advanced users can highlight the formula cell and the rows below it, then press &#8984;D (Mac) or Ctrl+D (Windows) to autofill the formula quickly.</li></ol><br/><table border='1' style='border-collapse:collapse; font-family: monospace; background-color:#eee;'><tr><th>Patient Name</th><th>Systolic BP (mmHg)</th></tr><tr><td>Audrey</td><td>77</td></tr><tr><td>Curtis</td><td>139</td></tr><tr><td>Ewa</td><td>119</td></tr><tr><td>Fadi</td><td>117</td></tr><tr><td>Hilary</td><td>100</td></tr><tr><td>Lindita</td><td>161</td></tr><tr><td>Olivia</td><td>125</td></tr><tr><td>Sarah</td><td>122</td></tr></table><br/><p><b>Question:</b> Which named patient has a Z-score between -2 and -1 standard deviations?</p>	Audrey with a BP of 77	Incorrect	Fadi with a BP of 117	Incorrect	Hilary with a BP of 100	Correct	Lindita with a BP of 161	Incorrect	Sarah with a BP of 122	Incorrect

The spreadsheet will automatically change B2 to B3 for the next row, then B4, and so on, so each patient's BP gets its own Z-score.</li><li><i>Pro Tip:</i> Advanced users can highlight the formula cell and the rows below it, then press &#8984;D (Mac) or Ctrl+D (Windows) to autofill the formula quickly.</li></ol><br/><table border='1' style='border-collapse:collapse; font-family: monospace; background-color:#eee;'><tr><th>Patient Name</th><th>Systolic BP (mmHg)</th></tr><tr><td>Awais</td><td>154</td></tr><tr><td>Curtis</td><td>116</td></tr><tr><td>Dalal</td><td>127</td></tr><tr><td>Joslyn</td><td>100</td></tr><tr><td>Monika</td><td>140</td></tr><tr><td>Neha</td><td>77</td></tr><tr><td>Sthefani</td><td>121</td></tr><tr><td>Yoana</td><td>118</td></tr></table><br/><p><b>Question:</b> Which named patient has a Z-score between -3 and -2 standard deviations?</p>	Awais with a BP of 154	Incorrect	Joslyn with a BP of 100	Incorrect	Monika with a BP of 140	Incorrect	Neha with a BP of 77	Correct	Sthefani with a BP of 121	Incorrect

The spreadsheet will automatically change B2 to B3 for the next row, then B4, and so on, so each patient's BP gets its own Z-score.</li><li><i>Pro Tip:</i> Advanced users can highlight the formula cell and the rows below it, then press &#8984;D (Mac) or Ctrl+D (Windows) to autofill the formula quickly.</li></ol><br/><table border='1' style='border-collapse:collapse; font-family: monospace; background-color:#eee;'><tr><th>Patient Name</th><th>Systolic BP (mmHg)</th></tr><tr><td>Alexis</td><td>127</td></tr><tr><td>Dongjin</td><td>101</td></tr><tr><td>Jasmine</td><td>156</td></tr><tr><td>Kenza</td><td>83</td></tr><tr><td>Liza</td><td>129</td></tr><tr><td>Rob</td><td>114</td></tr><tr><td>Simranjit</td><td>116</td></tr><tr><td>Vincent</td><td>139</td></tr></table><br/><p><b>Question:</b> Which named patient has a Z-score between +1 and +2 standard deviations?</p>	Alexis with a BP of 127	Incorrect	Jasmine with a BP of 156	Incorrect	Kenza with a BP of 83	Incorrect	Rob with a BP of 114	Incorrect	Vincent with a BP of 139	Correct

The spreadsheet will automatically change B2 to B3 for the next row, then B4, and so on, so each patient's BP gets its own Z-score.</li><li><i>Pro Tip:</i> Advanced users can highlight the formula cell and the rows below it, then press &#8984;D (Mac) or Ctrl+D (Windows) to autofill the formula quickly.</li></ol><br/><table border='1' style='border-collapse:collapse; font-family: monospace; background-color:#eee;'><tr><th>Patient Name</th><th>Systolic BP (mmHg)</th></tr><tr><td>Chris</td><td>140</td></tr><tr><td>Dao</td><td>80</td></tr><tr><td>Erika</td><td>113</td></tr><tr><td>Gloria</td><td>100</td></tr><tr><td>Jhelam</td><td>129</td></tr><tr><td>Kimberly</td><td>111</td></tr><tr><td>Lisa</td><td>128</td></tr><tr><td>Salume</td><td>154</td></tr></table><br/><p><b>Question:</b> Which named patient has a Z-score between -3 and -2 standard deviations?</p>	Chris with a BP of 140	Incorrect	Dao with a BP of 80	Correct	Gloria with a BP of 100	Incorrect	Lisa with a BP of 128	Incorrect	Salume with a BP of 154	Incorrect

The spreadsheet will automatically change B2 to B3 for the next row, then B4, and so on, so each patient's BP gets its own Z-score.</li><li><i>Pro Tip:</i> Advanced users can highlight the formula cell and the rows below it, then press &#8984;D (Mac) or Ctrl+D (Windows) to autofill the formula quickly.</li></ol><br/><table border='1' style='border-collapse:collapse; font-family: monospace; background-color:#eee;'><tr><th>Patient Name</th><th>Systolic BP (mmHg)</th></tr><tr><td>Ashley</td><td>126</td></tr><tr><td>Bhargavi</td><td>112</td></tr><tr><td>Dominique</td><td>154</td></tr><tr><td>Foram</td><td>139</td></tr><tr><td>Hafsa</td><td>115</td></tr><tr><td>Kingsley</td><td>77</td></tr><tr><td>Sam</td><td>100</td></tr><tr><td>Vishwa</td><td>128</td></tr></table><br/><p><b>Question:</b> Which named patient has a Z-score between -3 and -2 standard deviations?</p>	Dominique with a BP of 154	Incorrect	Hafsa with a BP of 115	Incorrect	Kingsley with a BP of 77	Correct	Sam with a BP of 100	Incorrect	Vishwa with a BP of 128	Incorrect

The spreadsheet will automatically change B2 to B3 for the next row, then B4, and so on, so each patient's BP gets its own Z-score.</li><li><i>Pro Tip:</i> Advanced users can highlight the formula cell and the rows below it, then press &#8984;D (Mac) or Ctrl+D (Windows) to autofill the formula quickly.</li></ol><br/><table border='1' style='border-collapse:collapse; font-family: monospace; background-color:#eee;'><tr><th>Patient Name</th><th>Systolic BP (mmHg)</th></tr><tr><td>Caitlyn</td><td>127</td></tr><tr><td>Divya</td><td>101</td></tr><tr><td>Etny</td><td>83</td></tr><tr><td>Firdose</td><td>115</td></tr><tr><td>Jullisa</td><td>160</td></tr><tr><td>Kaushal</td><td>140</td></tr><tr><td>Maria</td><td>124</td></tr><tr><td>Rob</td><td>115</td></tr></table><br/><p><b>Question:</b> Which named patient has a Z-score between -2 and -1 standard deviations?</p>	Divya with a BP of 101	Correct	Etny with a BP of 83	Incorrect	Firdose with a BP of 115	Incorrect	Jullisa with a BP of 160	Incorrect	Maria with a BP of 124	Incorrect

The spreadsheet will automatically change B2 to B3 for the next row, then B4, and so on, so each patient's BP gets its own Z-score.</li><li><i>Pro Tip:</i> Advanced users can highlight the formula cell and the rows below it, then press &#8984;D (Mac) or Ctrl+D (Windows) to autofill the formula quickly.</li></ol><br/><table border='1' style='border-collapse:collapse; font-family: monospace; background-color:#eee;'><tr><th>Patient Name</th><th>Systolic BP (mmHg)</th></tr><tr><td>Collin</td><td>101</td></tr><tr><td>Hasena</td><td>111</td></tr><tr><td>Jennifer</td><td>139</td></tr><tr><td>Kyron</td><td>80</td></tr><tr><td>Meleny</td><td>129</td></tr><tr><td>Rikita</td><td>129</td></tr><tr><td>Sonia</td><td>112</td></tr><tr><td>Yamini</td><td>161</td></tr></table><br/><p><b>Question:</b> Which named patient has a Z-score between +2 and +3 standard deviations?</p>	Collin with a BP of 101	Incorrect	Hasena with a BP of 111	Incorrect	Jennifer with a BP of 139	Incorrect	Kyron with a BP of 80	Incorrect	Yamini with a BP of 161	Correct

The spreadsheet will automatically change B2 to B3 for the next row, then B4, and so on, so each patient's BP gets its own Z-score.</li><li><i>Pro Tip:</i> Advanced users can highlight the formula cell and the rows below it, then press &#8984;D (Mac) or Ctrl+D (Windows) to autofill the formula quickly.</li></ol><br/><table border='1' style='border-collapse:collapse; font-family: monospace; background-color:#eee;'><tr><th>Patient Name</th><th>Systolic BP (mmHg)</th></tr><tr><td>Amtur</td><td>101</td></tr><tr><td>Calah</td><td>139</td></tr><tr><td>Jeffrey</td><td>158</td></tr><tr><td>Kiki</td><td>116</td></tr><tr><td>Leonardo</td><td>121</td></tr><tr><td>Mohammed</td><td>118</td></tr><tr><td>Sabrina</td><td>80</td></tr><tr><td>Veda</td><td>129</td></tr></table><br/><p><b>Question:</b> Which named patient has a Z-score between -2 and -1 standard deviations?</p>	Amtur with a BP of 101	Correct	Jeffrey with a BP of 158	Incorrect	Kiki with a BP of 116	Incorrect	Mohammed with a BP of 118	Incorrect	Sabrina with a BP of 80	Incorrect

The spreadsheet will automatically change B2 to B3 for the next row, then B4, and so on, so each patient's BP gets its own Z-score.</li><li><i>Pro Tip:</i> Advanced users can highlight the formula cell and the rows below it, then press &#8984;D (Mac) or Ctrl+D (Windows) to autofill the formula quickly.</li></ol><br/><table border='1' style='border-collapse:collapse; font-family: monospace; background-color:#eee;'><tr><th>Patient Name</th><th>Systolic BP (mmHg)</th></tr><tr><td>Aashi</td><td>139</td></tr><tr><td>Gavin</td><td>130</td></tr><tr><td>Harin</td><td>113</td></tr><tr><td>Kiara</td><td>113</td></tr><tr><td>Mary</td><td>86</td></tr><tr><td>Rutuja</td><td>100</td></tr><tr><td>Shraddha</td><td>152</td></tr><tr><td>Vamshi</td><td>124</td></tr></table><br/><p><b>Question:</b> Which named patient has a Z-score between +1 and +2 standard deviations?</p>	Aashi with a BP of 139	Correct	Kiara with a BP of 113	Incorrect	Mary with a BP of 86	Incorrect	Rutuja with a BP of 100	Incorrect	Shraddha with a BP of 152	Incorrect

The spreadsheet will automatically change B2 to B3 for the next row, then B4, and so on, so each patient's BP gets its own Z-score.</li><li><i>Pro Tip:</i> Advanced users can highlight the formula cell and the rows below it, then press &#8984;D (Mac) or Ctrl+D (Windows) to autofill the formula quickly.</li></ol><br/><table border='1' style='border-collapse:collapse; font-family: monospace; background-color:#eee;'><tr><th>Patient Name</th><th>Systolic BP (mmHg)</th></tr><tr><td>Anjali</td><td>121</td></tr><tr><td>Clarke</td><td>82</td></tr><tr><td>Esmeralda</td><td>127</td></tr><tr><td>Jocelyn</td><td>101</td></tr><tr><td>Krupa</td><td>161</td></tr><tr><td>Liza</td><td>117</td></tr><tr><td>Maggie</td><td>140</td></tr><tr><td>Norhan</td><td>112</td></tr></table><br/><p><b>Question:</b> Which named patient has a Z-score between -2 and -1 standard deviations?</p>	Anjali with a BP of 121	Incorrect	Clarke with a BP of 82	Incorrect	Jocelyn with a BP of 101	Correct	Krupa with a BP of 161	Incorrect	Liza with a BP of 117	Incorrect

The spreadsheet will automatically change B2 to B3 for the next row, then B4, and so on, so each patient's BP gets its own Z-score.</li><li><i>Pro Tip:</i> Advanced users can highlight the formula cell and the rows below it, then press &#8984;D (Mac) or Ctrl+D (Windows) to autofill the formula quickly.</li></ol><br/><table border='1' style='border-collapse:collapse; font-family: monospace; background-color:#eee;'><tr><th>Patient Name</th><th>Systolic BP (mmHg)</th></tr><tr><td>Aditi</td><td>127</td></tr><tr><td>Brenda</td><td>116</td></tr><tr><td>Ivanna</td><td>155</td></tr><tr><td>Joel</td><td>140</td></tr><tr><td>Kher</td><td>79</td></tr><tr><td>Lakshmidhar</td><td>115</td></tr><tr><td>Prit</td><td>129</td></tr><tr><td>Srilatha</td><td>101</td></tr></table><br/><p><b>Question:</b> Which named patient has a Z-score between -3 and -2 standard deviations?</p>	Ivanna with a BP of 155	Incorrect	Joel with a BP of 140	Incorrect	Kher with a BP of 79	Correct	Prit with a BP of 129	Incorrect	Srilatha with a BP of 101	Incorrect

The spreadsheet will automatically change B2 to B3 for the next row, then B4, and so on, so each patient's BP gets its own Z-score.</li><li><i>Pro Tip:</i> Advanced users can highlight the formula cell and the rows below it, then press &#8984;D (Mac) or Ctrl+D (Windows) to autofill the formula quickly.</li></ol><br/><table border='1' style='border-collapse:collapse; font-family: monospace; background-color:#eee;'><tr><th>Patient Name</th><th>Systolic BP (mmHg)</th></tr><tr><td>Alyaa</td><td>156</td></tr><tr><td>Foramben</td><td>101</td></tr><tr><td>Hafsa</td><td>114</td></tr><tr><td>Jocelyn</td><td>79</td></tr><tr><td>Khushi</td><td>140</td></tr><tr><td>Mansi</td><td>115</td></tr><tr><td>Sony</td><td>128</td></tr><tr><td>Valbhi</td><td>121</td></tr></table><br/><p><b>Question:</b> Which named patient has a Z-score between -2 and -1 standard deviations?</p>	Alyaa with a BP of 156	Incorrect	Foramben with a BP of 101	Correct	Jocelyn with a BP of 79	Incorrect	Khushi with a BP of 140	Incorrect	Sony with a BP of 128	Incorrect
